  If Black plays `c5e3`, it attacks the pawn but the Rook remains.

  If I play `d1f1`, I take a rook.

  The bishop at c5 is eyeing e3.

  The Queen at d1 is dominating the first rank.

  If I take the Rook (`d1f1`), I simply eliminate White's most active piece.
